### Full Text

````text
# Requested Channel Not Available Message Covering SIRIUSXM® Preset Icons (A18060F)

## REQUESTED CHANNEL NOT AVAILABLE MESSAGE COVERING SIRIUSXM® PRESET ICONS

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016-18 Civic; 2017-18 CR-V

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim

2016-18 | Civic | with SiriusXM Radio

2017-18 | CR-V | with SiriusXM Radio

Got a vehicle with the message Requested Channel Not Available covering the SiriusXM preset icons? We're aware of this issue and have it under investigation.

This message only comes up when you select a SiriusXM station from the preset icons. When selected, the station starts playing, but the message covers those icons. This doesn't happen when using other means such as the Channel icons.

Until there's a solution to this issue, there's nothing you can do to fix it, so don't replace any parts. Instead, try this procedure. It should get rid of that message, but keep in mind this is just temporary, so the message may come back.

- From the SOURCE screen, select MENU, Setting, Multiple Channel Mix Preset , then ON .

- Select OK .

- Select the BACK icon to go back to the audio source screen.

- Using the Channel icons, select a different SiriusXM (music, no talk) station that's not already preset, and save it to one of the preset icons. You'll then see the message Tunemix allows multiple channels to be combined on one preset. Would you like to combine this channel with the current preset or replace it?. Select Combine.

- Select a different preset icon, and make sure the message doesn't come back.

Be sure to pass this info along to the service advisors so they can educate their customers.
````

## Chunk 10907: Sound Cuts Out For Several Seconds While Playing SIRIUSXM® Radio (A17110E)

### Full Text

````text
# Sound Cuts Out For Several Seconds While Playing SIRIUSXM® Radio (A17110E)

## SOUND CUTS OUT FOR SEVERAL SECONDS WHILE PLAYING SIRIUSXM® RADIO

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | All Models

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

All models with SiriusXM Radio

While playing SiriusXM Radio, if the sound cuts out for several seconds when driving through the same open area, the likely cause could be interference from local cellular towers. And because of differences in XM antenna design, the amount of that interference can vary from model to model.

If you get a vehicle in for this issue and you find from your troubleshooting that cellular interference is the likely cause, don't try replacing any parts; it won't make a difference.
````

## Chunk 10908: Audio Unit/Audio-Navigation Unit Stuck In Factory Diagnostic Mode? (A17080C)

- Title: Audio Unit/Audio-Navigation Unit Stuck In Factory Diagnostic Mode? (A17080C)
- Source path: `pages\26626.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_e984ba5ad30e`
- Images: `images\G11602354.png`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\26682.html`, `pages\18927.html`, `pages\18983.html`

### Full Text

````text
# Audio Unit/Audio-Navigation Unit Stuck In Factory Diagnostic Mode? (A17080C)

## AUDIO UNIT/AUDIO-NAVIGATION UNIT STUCK IN FACTORY DIAGNOSTIC MODE?

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2005 and later models

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

2005 and later models

After installing a reman audio unit/audio-navigation unit or a brand new one, are you seeing this screen (or something very similar to it) and it's staying up even after cycling the ignition?

That's because that unit is in the factory diagnostic mode . This mode lets the factory check that everything is plugged in and working right as the vehicle moves down the production line. At the end of the line, before the vehicle leaves the factory, the unit is taken out of this mode and returned to the normal market mode.

Exiting the factory diagnostic mode takes just a few steps, but unless you do them exactly right, the unit will remain stuck in that mode. We started adding exit mode procedures a number of years back to the PDI of the navigation system bulletins, and in the last couple of years, to the basic PDI bulletins. You'll also find them in the service information.

Unfortunately though, not every affected vehicle may be covered. If you can't find the procedure in SIS for the vehicle you're working on, try these steps; they should let you exit the mode:

- Select Start Diag.

- Press all of the buttons on the steering wheel that you see on the screen. The screen icons should turn green.

- Make a loud sound (like snapping your fingers or clapping your hands) next to the microphone to complete the mic test.

- When you've completed all of the tests, the screen icons will be green. Select Exit Diag to exit the mode.

- Turn the ignition to OFF, then to ON, to make sure the In Line Diag screen doesn't reappear.

Of course, if you still can't exit the mode, then call Tech Line. One of their agents will gladly help you.
````

````text
# Using The GPS And Satellite Test Antennas (AJA05118)

## USING THE GPS AND SATELLITE TEST ANTENNAS

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | All Models

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

General information

### REVISION SUMMARY

This job aid has been extensively revised; American Honda recommends you read the entire bulletin.

### INTRODUCTION

Troubleshooting either a GPS antenna problem (navigation GPS signal missing, clock not setting) or XM antenna problem (Check Antenna or No Signal message, poor XM reception)? Keep in mind there are two test antennas that we auto shipped to every dealer that can help make short work of this.

- GPS Test Antenna (NUD420391)

- Satellite Radio Test Antenna (NUD420392)

Both of these antennas are available from the Honda Tool and Equipment program if you need to order a replacement or extras for your shop.

This job aid shows you how to use these test antennas. It covers these two troubleshooting areas:

- GPS Positioning or Clock Setting (Non-Navigation Audio Units with GPS Antenna)

- Check Antenna or No Signal Messages, Poor XM Reception

You might be wondering why vehicles without navigation have a GPS antenna. Many current and most future vehicles will use it to set the clock.

### GPS POSITIONING (NAVI) OR CLOCK SETTING (NON-NAVI AUDIO UNITS WITH A GPS ANTENNA)

- Disconnect the GPS antenna from the back of the navigation unit, audio-navigation unit or audio unit, and plug in the GPS test antenna. Leave the antenna in place. Only disconnect the connector from the unit at this time.

- Place the GPS test antenna on the roof, park the vehicle outside away from tall buildings, trees, or high powered electrical lines, or drive the vehicle as necessary and see if the issue is now resolved. Is the issue resolved? YES - Go to step 3. NO - Substitute a known-good navigation unit, audio-navigation or audio unit, and retest.

Is the issue resolved?

YES - Go to step 3.

NO - Substitute a known-good navigation unit, audio-navigation or audio unit, and retest.

- Move the test antenna next to the area of the dashboard or rear deck that is directly over the location of the original GPS antenna, and repeat step 2 . Did the problem return? YES - There is interference in the area of the GPS antenna blocking the GPS signal. Possible causes can be controls units like the gauge control module, HVAC, or other C/Us in the area of the antenna or the dashboard wire harness. Also, look for aftermarket devices hidden near the GPS antenna or electronic toll road devises directly above the GPS antenna. (cell phone chargers, Bluetooth devices, DC to AC inverters) NO - Replace the GPS antenna.

Did the problem return?

YES - There is interference in the area of the GPS antenna blocking the GPS signal. Possible causes can be controls units like the gauge control module, HVAC, or other C/Us in the area of the antenna or the dashboard wire harness. Also, look for aftermarket devices hidden near the GPS antenna or electronic toll road devises directly above the GPS antenna. (cell phone chargers, Bluetooth devices, DC to AC inverters)

NO - Replace the GPS antenna.

### XM CHECK ANTENNA OR NO SIGNAL MESSAGES OR POOR XM RECEPTION

- For a Check Antenna message, go to step 3 .

- For a No Signal message or poor reception, go to step 7 .

- Unplug the XM antenna from the rear of the XM receiver or the back of the audio-navigation unit if the XM receiver is built-in.

- Plug the XM test antenna into the rear of the XM receiver or into the back of the audio-navigation unit if the XM receiver is built-in, and place the test antenna on the roof. Then, retest the vehicle under the same conditions. Is the Check Antenna message gone? YES - Go to step 5. NO - Replace the XM receiver (or the audio-navigation unit if the XM receiver is built-in. The unit is defective internally.

Is the Check Antenna message gone?

YES - Go to step 5.

NO - Replace the XM receiver (or the audio-navigation unit if the XM receiver is built-in. The unit is defective internally.

- Unplug the XM test antenna, and plug in the XM antenna connector.

- Substitute a known-good XM antenna, and retest. Is the Check Antenna message gone? YES - Replace the original XM antenna. The antenna is defective internally. NO - Replace the XM coaxial antenna lead(s) as necessary. The coaxial lead(s) are defective.

Is the Check Antenna message gone?

YES - Replace the original XM antenna. The antenna is defective internally.
````

### Full Text

````text
# Popping Or No Sound From Speakers (17-016)

## POPPING OR NO SOUND FROM SPEAKERS

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016 Civic

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim | VIN Range

2016 | Civic | Touring | ALL

### SYMPTOM

Popping or no sound from the speakers.

### CORRECTIVE ACTION

Update the audio unit software.

### TOOL INFORMATION

Part Name | Part Number | Quantity

2016 Civic (Yellow/Blue) USB Update Device | 07AAU-TBAA3A0 | 1

### WARRANTY CLAIM INFORMATION

The normal warranty applies.

Operation Number | Description | Flat Rate Time | Defect Code | Symptom Code | Template ID | Failed Part Number

053011 | Update the audio unit software. | 0.3 hr | 03214 | 03217 | 17-016A | 39186-TBA-A01

Skill Level: Repair Technician

### REPAIR PROCEDURE

- Connect a battery charger or a fully charged jumper battery to the vehicle, and leave it connected during the entire update procedure to maintain a steady voltage.

- Insert the USB update device into the USB port in the center console.

- Turn the ignition to ON.

- Allow the audio system to boot up (about 20 seconds), then press and hold the MENU icon, AUDIO icon, and Display Mode button at the same time until the diagnostic screen appears.

- Select Detail Information & Setting.

- Select Functional Setup.

- Select AMP Repro.

- Select Check.

- Select Yes to start the update. NOTE: Once the update begins, no action is needed. The update will take about 15 minutes. You do not need to stay with the vehicle.

- When the update is complete, the confirmation screen will appear. Do not remove the USB update device.

- Turn the ignition to OFF, and leave it off for at least 90 seconds.

- Turn the ignition to ON, and do steps 3 through 8 to confirm the DSP software version was updated. SOFTWARE INFORMATION Software Software Version SYS uCOM 802A DSP 3.60 EQ DATA AMA1 A setup of a system TBAA

Software | Software Version

SYS uCOM | 802A

DSP | 3.60

EQ DATA | AMA1

A setup of a system | TBAA

- Turn the ignition to OFF, and remove the USB update device.

END
````

- Title: Display Audio Screen Problems? (A15120G)

- Images: none

### Full Text

````text
# Display Audio Screen Problems? (A15120G)

## DISPLAY AUDIO SCREEN PROBLEMS?

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016 Civic

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

2016 Civic

If the Display Audio (DA) screen has display-related problems, they're coming from the center display assembly, not the audio unit or audio-navigation unit.

Typical display-related problems include the following:

- Blank screen

- Flickering or flashing screen

- Discolored screen

- No back lighting

- No touch response

To fix such problems, don't order a reman audio unit or audio-navigation unit. A replacement center display assembly is all you need.
````

````text
# Recommended Sealers And Adhesives For Collision And Body Repairs (A18-134)

## RECOMMENDED SEALERS AND ADHESIVES FOR COLLISION AND BODY REPAIRS

## SERVICE CAMPAIGN BULLETIN

HONDA: | All Models

### REVISION SUMMARY

Under the 2K Seam Sealers and Liquid Applied Sound Deadening tables, the information has been updated.

### RECOMMENDED SEALERS AND ADHESIVES

- Always follow Honda's applicable body repair manual information for location of adhesives, sealers, foams and corrosion protection materials.

- Always follow the product manufacturer's recommendations for application of adhesives, sealers, foams and corrosion protection materials.

Work Time/Speed for Metal Bonding Adhesives

Fast | 5-10 min

E | Epoxy

M | MSP or Modified Silane Polymer

S | STP or Silane Terminated Polymer

U | Urethane
````

## Chunk 10918: CAPLESS Fuel Filler (Mis-Fuel Inhibit Device) Leaks When Refueling Vehicle (18-014)

- Title: CAPLESS Fuel Filler (Mis-Fuel Inhibit Device) Leaks When Refueling Vehicle (18-014)
- Source path: `pages\26637.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_5b6ebea1853e`
- Images: `images\G12051665.png`, `images\G12051666.png`, `images\G12051667.jpeg`, `images\G12051668.jpeg`, `images\G12051669.jpeg`, `images\G12051670.jpeg`, `images\G12051671.jpeg`, `images\G12051672.jpeg`, `images\G12051673.jpeg`, `images\G12051674.jpeg`, `images\G12051675.jpeg`, `images\G12051676.jpeg`, `images\G12051677.jpeg`, `images\G12051678.jpeg`

### Full Text

````text
# CAPLESS Fuel Filler (Mis-Fuel Inhibit Device) Leaks When Refueling Vehicle (18-014)

## CAPLESS FUEL FILLER (MIS-FUEL INHIBIT DEVICE) LEAKS WHEN REFUELING VEHICLE

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016-17 Civic; 2016-18 Civic; 2017-18 Civic

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim | VIN Range

2016-17 | Civic | 4-door | ALL

2016-18 | Civic | 2-door | ALL

2017-18 | Civic | 5-door | ALL

### REVISION SUMMARY

- Under AFFECTED VEHICLES, Civic 5-door was added.

- Under WARRANTY CLAIM INFORMATION, new Template IDs for Civic 5-door was added.

- Under PARTS INFORMATION, Civic 5-door part number was added.

- Under REPAIR PROCEDURE, Civic 5-door pictures and steps were added.

### SYMPTOM

Fuel leaks or overflows from the filler neck while refueling.

### PROBABLE CAUSE

The auto shut-off feature at the gas pump may be delayed and cause fuel to leak at the filler neck. The capless fuel shutter may also allow excess fuel to leak at the filler neck.

### CORRECTIVE ACTION

Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel filler lid cover and apply the fuel lid label (if applicable). Refer the customer to page 112 of the Owner's Guide for refueling precautions.

### WARRANTY CLAIM INFORMATION

The normal warranty applies.

Operation Number | Description | Flat Rate Time | Defect Code | Symptom Code | Template ID | Failed Part Number

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover (2-door non-Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18014C | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover (4-door non-Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18014D | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover and the fuel lid label (2-door Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18014E | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover and the fuel lid label (4-door Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18014F | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover (5-door LX, EX, and EX-L models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18014G | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover and the fuel lid label (5-door Sport, Sport Touring, and Type R models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18014H | 17060-TBA-L01

Skill Level: Repair Technician

### PARTS INFORMATION

Part Name | Part Number | Quantity

Fuel Filler Caution Label (Si, Sport, Sport Touring, and Type R models only) | 17669-RL5-A00 | 1

Fuel Filler Lid Cover (5-door) | 17666-TGG-A01 | 1

Fuel Filler Lid Cover (2-door) | 17666-TBG-A01 | 1

Fuel Filler Lid Cover (4-door) | 17666-TBA-A01 | 1

Capless Shutter Set (Mis-Fuel Inhibit Device) | 17671-TBA-A02 | 1

### REPAIR PROCEDURE

- Remove the left rear inner fender as needed.

- Remove the fuel fill door lock actuator.

- Remove the fuel fill door adapter.

- Remove the mis-fuel inhibit device by breaking the 6 lock tabs.

- Pry up the lock tab and remove the mis-fuel inhibit device.

- Install the new mis-fuel inhibit device.

- Re-install all removed parts.

Civic 4-door

- On Si models only : Apply the fuel lid label to the new fuel lid cover as shown. NOTE: Make sure the new fuel lid cover is clean and grease-free, especially on the surface where the label will be applied.

- Align the hooks on the top edge of the fuel lid cover to slots on the fuel door as shown.

- Press the bottom of the fuel lid cover against the fuel door until both locking tabs click. This indicates the fuel lid cover is securely and correctly installed.

- Make sure the fuel door closes and latches without contact or interference from the mis-fuel inhibit device and surrounding area. The gap between the door and the surrounding quarter panel should remain consistent.

- Make sure the fuel door release works correctly when using the driver's door switch.

Civic 2-door

- On Si models only : Apply the fuel lid label to the new fuel lid cover as shown. NOTE: Make sure the new fuel lid cover is clean and grease-free, especially on the surface where the label will be applied.

- Align the hook on the right side edge of the fuel lid cover to the fuel door as shown.

- Press the left of the fuel lid cover against the fuel door until both locking tabs click. This indicates the fuel lid cover is securely and correctly installed.
````

````text
# CAPLESS Fuel Shutter Set (Mis-Fuel Inhibit Device) Is Frozen (18-012)

## CAPLESS FUEL SHUTTER SET (MIS-FUEL INHIBIT DEVICE) IS FROZEN

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016-2017 Civic; 2016-2018 Civic; 2017-2018 Civic

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim | VIN Range

2016-17 | Civic | 4-door | ALL

2016-18 | Civic | 2-door | ALL

2017-18 | Civic | 5-door | ALL

### REVISION SUMMARY

- Under AFFECTED VEHICLES, Civic 5-door was added.

- Under WARRANTY CLAIM INFORMATION, new Template IDs for Civic 5-door was added.

- Under PARTS INFORMATION, Civic 5-door part number was added.

- Under REPAIR PROCEDURE, Civic 5-door pictures and steps were added.

### SYMPTOM

The customer is unable to insert the fuel nozzle into the filler neck and fuel the vehicle in cold weather.

### PROBABLE CAUSE

Water or moisture has entered the fueling area and frozen the capless fuel shutter set in the fuel filler neck.

### CORRECTIVE ACTION

Replace the capless shutter set. Install the fuel filler lid and apply the fuel filler caution label (if applicable).

### WARRANTY CLAIM INFORMATION

The normal warranty applies.

Operation Number | Description | Flat Rate Time | Defect Code | Symptom Code | Template ID | Failed Part Number

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover (2-door non-Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18012C | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover (4-door non-Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18012D | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover and the fuel lid label (2-door Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18012E | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover and the fuel lid label (4-door Si models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18012F | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover (5-door LX, EX, and EX-L models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18012G | 17060-TBA-L01

3101U2 | Replace the capless fuel shutter set. Install the fuel lid cover and the fuel lid label (5-door Sport, Sport Touring, and Type R models only). | 0.7 hr | 03214 | 03217 | A18012H | 17060-TBA-L01

Skill Level: Repair Technician

### PARTS INFORMATION

Part Name | Part Number | Quantity

Fuel Filler Caution Label (Si, Sport, Sport Touring, and Type R models only) | 17669-RL5-A00 | 1

Fuel Filler Lid Cover (5-door) | 17666-TGG-A01 | 1

Fuel Filler Lid Cover (2-door) | 17666-TBG-A01 | 1

Fuel Filler Lid Cover (4-door) | 17666-TBA-A01 | 1

Capless Shutter Set (Mis-Fuel Inhibit Device) | 17671-TBA-A02 | 1

### REPAIR PROCEDURE

- Remove the left rear inner fender as needed.

- Remove the fuel fill door lock actuator.

- Remove the fuel fill door adapter.

- Remove the mis-fuel inhibit device by breaking the 6 lock tabs.

- Pry up the lock tab and remove the mis-fuel inhibit device.

- Install the new mis-fuel inhibit device.

- Re-install all removed parts.

Civic 4-door

- On Si models only : Apply the fuel lid label to the new fuel lid cover as shown. NOTE: Make sure the new fuel lid cover is clean and grease-free, especially on the surface where the label will be applied.

- Align the hooks on the top edge of the fuel lid cover to slots on the fuel door as shown.

- Press the bottom of the fuel lid cover against the fuel door until both locking tabs click. This indicates the fuel lid cover is securely and correctly installed.

- Make sure the fuel door closes and latches without contact or interference from the mis-fuel inhibit device and surrounding area. The gap between the door and the surrounding quarter panel should remain consistent.

- Make sure the fuel door release works correctly when using the driver's door switch.

Civic 2-door

- On Si models only : Apply the fuel lid label to the new fuel lid cover as shown. NOTE: Make sure the new fuel lid cover is clean and grease-free, especially on the surface where the label will be applied.

- Align the hook on the right side edge of the fuel lid cover to the fuel door as shown.

- Press the left of the fuel lid cover against the fuel door until both locking tabs click. This indicates the fuel lid cover is securely and correctly installed.

- Make sure the fuel door closes and latches without contact or interference from the mis-fuel inhibit device and surrounding area.
````

### Full Text

````text
# Service Manual Update: Fuel Filler Main Flap Unit Replacement (16-009)

## SERVICE MANUAL UPDATE: FUEL FILLER MAIN FLAP UNIT REPLACEMENT

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016 Civic; 2016 Pilot

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim | VIN Range

2016 | Civic | ALL | ALL

2016 | Pilot | ALL | ALL

### REVISION SUMMARY

Under AFFECTED VEHICLES, Pilot has been added to the model list. There are also changes under PROCEDURE to reflect model differences.

### BACKGROUND

The service information is missing the replacement procedures for the main flap unit.

### TOOL INFORMATION

Part Name | Part Number | Quantity

Capless Wrench | 07AAF-TBAA100 | 1

Capless Flapper Retainer | 07AAF-TBAA200 | 1

Capless Pick | 07AAF-TBAA300 | 1

### PROCEDURE

#### Removal

- Remove the fuel fill door adapter (Civic only). Refer to the service information.

- Prepare the mis-fuel inhibit device for removal. Civic : Break the six lock tabs. Pilot : Break the four lock tabs then turn the mis-fuel inhibit device clockwise.

- Civic : Break the six lock tabs.

- Pilot : Break the four lock tabs then turn the mis-fuel inhibit device clockwise.

- Remove the mis-fuel inhibit device. NOTE: Never reuse the mis-fuel inhibit device. Civic : Pry up the lock tab, then pull the mis-fuel inhibit device straight off the filler neck. Pilot : Pull the mis-fuel inhibit device off the filler neck and toward the rear of the vehicle.

- Civic : Pry up the lock tab, then pull the mis-fuel inhibit device straight off the filler neck.

- Pilot : Pull the mis-fuel inhibit device off the filler neck and toward the rear of the vehicle.

- Set the capless wrench and slightly turn the main flap unit counterclockwise. NOTE: The main flap unit may only move a small amount. This is normal.

- Remove the capless wrench and install the flapper retainer onto the main flap unit as shown to keep the flap open.

- Insert the capless pick under the lock tab and release the lock by turning the pick counterclockwise. NOTE: The lock tab is unlocked when it folds inside the filler neck opening.

- Set the capless wrench onto the main flap unit as shown. NOTE: It may be necessary to move the flapper retainer slightly to install the capless wrench.

- Turn the main flap unit clockwise until it stops, then remove it. NOTE: If the lock tab was disengaged properly, the main flap unit should turn easily. If you feel resistance, remove the capless wrench and confirm that the lock tab was disengaged.

#### Installation

- Position the new main flap unit onto the filler neck with the top arrow at the 1 o'clock position.

- Install the main flap unit by doing the following: Set the capless wrench onto the main flap unit. While pressing down on the capless wrench, turn the main flap unit counterclockwise until you hear a click. The arrow on the main flap unit should face up. Slightly turn the capless wrench clockwise to confirm that the lock tab is engaged. The main flap unit should not turn.

- Set the capless wrench onto the main flap unit.

- While pressing down on the capless wrench, turn the main flap unit counterclockwise until you hear a click. The arrow on the main flap unit should face up.

- Slightly turn the capless wrench clockwise to confirm that the lock tab is engaged. The main flap unit should not turn.

- Install the new mis-fuel inhibit device by pressing into place until the lock tabs engage. Civic : Pilot :

- Civic :

- Pilot :

- Reinstall the remaining removed parts.
````

## Chunk 10923: Improper Body Noise Repair Can Be Costly! (A16020B)

- Title: Improper Body Noise Repair Can Be Costly! (A16020B)

- Chunk ID: `chunk_f4168ff7e324`
- Images: none
- Duplicate sources: `pages\26646.html`, `pages\18942.html`, `pages\18947.html`

### Full Text

````text
# Improper Body Noise Repair Can Be Costly! (A16020B)

## IMPROPER BODY NOISE REPAIR CAN BE COSTLY!

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | All Models

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

General Information

### INTRODUCTION

Applying random heat or welds to the body structure rarely fixes a body noise problem. In fact, on newer vehicles, which use more and more high-strength steel (HSS) and ultra-high-strength steel (UHSS) to meet increasing collision protection and fuel efficiency requirements, doing this can actually cause structural damage that can be very expensive to repair.

UHSS is any steel with a tensile strength of 980 MPa or higher. Our vehicles currently use 980 MPa and 1,500 MPa steel parts. You'll find this steel most often in the front upper and lower pillars, the center pillar (B-pillar), and the side sill area, but you may also find it in other areas on certain models.

Keep in mind that UHSS parts can be tough to nearly impossible to bend, dent, or otherwise adjust, to repair a body noise problem. They can't be heated, cut, and/or MAG welded without a major loss in strength. Doing so could result in an unsafe repair because in a future collision, these parts may not work as designed, thus reducing occupant crash protection. Also, with UHSS parts, there's no way to visually inspect or otherwise determine if they've been damaged/weakened by heat application or welding or the extent of such damage.

Many body shops aren't aware of these structural changes and a large number of them don't have access to body service info and/or aren't used to reading it before starting repairs. The tendency is to just do what what's always been done.

### KNOW YOUR RESOURCES

To avoid improper body noise repairs, you must know the body construction and tensile strength of the steel parts in the area of the noise. Two resources are available in SIS as well as in Service Express if you're a subscriber: the body repair manual and the Body Repair News .

- Body Repair Manual: This manual is posted for every vehicle model series. The General Information section includes body construction pages showing illustrations of each body structural part and its tensile strength.

- Body Repair News: This newsletter is posted for nearly every vehicle using 1,500 MPa steel construction. It summarizes new body and vehicle technology that may affect collision and other body repairs.

To access these resources, just do a search by vehicle and enter keyword BRM. This will bring up both the body repair manual as well as the Body Repair News for that vehicle.

### TIPS TO AVOID IMPROPER BODY NOISE REPAIRS

- Make good use of the STEELMAN® ChassisEAR™ (T/N JSP-SM06600). This required special tool lets you electronically pinpoint sounds in up to six different locations at a time. It's ideal for finding the source of body creaks, rattles, and squeaks during a test-drive. For more info, check out S/B 00-063, Steelman ChassisEAR™ Diagnostic Tool .

- Don't send a vehicle with a body noise problem to a body shop without first contacting Tech Line. We highly recommend that warranty body repairs be done by a ProFirst Certified Collision Repair Facility. These shops have the tools, training, and service info needed to properly repair Honda vehicles.

- Print a copy of this ServiceNews article along with the applicable Body Repair News and send them to the body shop along with the vehicle. Never assume the body shop has or will refer to the right service info.

- Never apply heat, such as from a torch, to UHSS body parts during body noise repair. Any repair needing heat application or welding must be reviewed and approved by Tech Line to make sure it won't affect the body structure.

- If you're considering welding to repair a body noise problem, you must check the applicable body repair manual and Body Repair News to determine if the steel panels in that body area can be MAG (MIG) welded.

- The body repair manual covers the welding restrictions for UHSS. These restrictions have nothing to do with technician skill, but rather physics and metallurgy. Because of them, body noise repair on newer vehicles could take a lot of structural disassembly to properly complete. When planning a repair, you should carefully consider the possible amount of body intrusion.

- Always keep your DPSM in the loop on a body noise situation to help in his or her decision-making analysis.

### WHAT TO DO IF THERE'S BEEN IMPROPER BODY NOISE REPAIR
````

## Chunk 10924: Improper Body Noise Repair Can Be Costly! (A16020B)

- Title: Improper Body Noise Repair Can Be Costly! (A16020B)
- Source path: `pages\26641.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_576ab7b80aad`
- Images: none
- Duplicate sources: `pages\26646.html`, `pages\18942.html`, `pages\18947.html`

### Full Text

````text
ke sure it won't affect the body structure.

- If you're considering welding to repair a body noise problem, you must check the applicable body repair manual and Body Repair News to determine if the steel panels in that body area can be MAG (MIG) welded.

- The body repair manual covers the welding restrictions for UHSS. These restrictions have nothing to do with technician skill, but rather physics and metallurgy. Because of them, body noise repair on newer vehicles could take a lot of structural disassembly to properly complete. When planning a repair, you should carefully consider the possible amount of body intrusion.

- Always keep your DPSM in the loop on a body noise situation to help in his or her decision-making analysis.

### WHAT TO DO IF THERE'S BEEN IMPROPER BODY NOISE REPAIR

Any UHSS part that's had MAG welding done to it during noise repair attempts must be replaced at factory seams according to the applicable body repair manual. This can mean some very expensive and intrusive repairs to the vehicle body. Notify your DPSM of the situation.

Keep in mind damage caused by improper body noise repairs isn't covered by warranty. Regardless of whether a dealer or sublet body shop did them, the dealer is responsible for the cost of all corrective repairs.
````

- Title: Service Manual Update: Fuel Tank Unit Removal (16-010)
- Source path: `pages\26642.html`

- Images: `images\G10220236.png`, `images\G10220237.png`, `images\G10220238.png`, `images\G10220239.png`, `images\G10220240.png`, `images\G10220241.png`

### Full Text

````text
# Service Manual Update: Fuel Tank Unit Removal (16-010)

## SERVICE MANUAL UPDATE: FUEL TANK UNIT REMOVAL

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016 Civic

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim | VIN Range

2016 | Civic | ALL | ALL

### BACKGROUND

If fuel enters the inner fuel vent tube, a DTC will set and the inner fuel vent tube must be replaced. A new required special tool, which is a plug, will protect the inner fuel vent tube while servicing the fuel tank unit.

Whenever you are doing the following procedures, you must use the required ORVR service plug to prevent fuel from entering the inner fuel vent tube:

- Removing and installing the fuel tank unit.

- Removing and installing the fuel tank assembly.

- Testing the fuel tank unit.

- Draining fuel from the fuel tank.

### TOOL INFORMATION

Tool Name | Tool Number | Quantity

ORVR Service Plug | 07AAK-TBAA100 | 1

### PROCEDURE

#### Plug Installation

- Do steps 1 through 7 of the Fuel Tank Unit Removal and Installation procedure. Refer to the service information for instructions.

- Hook the plug's lanyard on the child safety seat hook as shown. Set the plug near the fuel tank unit opening. NOTE: Do not damage any part of the plug's surface. Damage to the plug may cause fuel leaks into the inner fuel vent tube. Use the provided protective cap when the plug is not in use. If there is damage, discard the plug and order a new one.

- Disconnect the inner fuel vent tube from the fuel tank unit and hold it. Do not allow the tube to drop into the fuel tank.

- Install the plug onto the inner fuel vent tube. Make sure the plug is fully seated by holding the tube and gently pulling on the plug lanyard. The plug should not come off.

- Carefully lower the tube into the fuel tank, making sure the plug lanyard is free and away from the fuel tank unit. NOTE: It is OK for the tube to be submerged in the fuel.

- Remove the fuel tank unit.

#### Plug Removal

- Grab the plug lanyard and pull the inner fuel vent tube up until it is close to the fuel tank unit opening.

- Move the fuel tank unit aside, allowing space to remove the plug from the tube. NOTE: Do not allow the tube to fall inside the tank. If fuel enters the inner fuel vent tube, a DTC will set and the inner fuel vent tube will need to be replaced

- Remove the plug away from the tank opening and connect the tube to the fuel tank unit.

- Remove the plug from the child safety seat hook and install the provided protective cap.

- Install the fuel tank unit into the fuel tank according to the service information.
````

### Full Text

````text
# Service Manual Update: Fuel Filler Main Flap Unit Replacement (15-083)

## SERVICE MANUAL UPDATE: FUEL FILLER MAIN FLAP UNIT REPLACEMENT

## TECHNICAL SERVICE BULLETIN

HONDA: | 2016 Civic

### AFFECTED VEHICLES

Year | Model | Trim | VIN Range

2016 | Civic | ALL | ALL

### BACKGROUND

The service information is missing the replacement procedures for the main flap unit.

### TOOL INFORMATION

Part Name | Part Number | Quantity

Capless Wrench | 07AAF-TBAA100 | 1

Capless Flapper Retainer | 07AAF-TBAA200 | 1

Capless Pick | 07AAF-TBAA300 | 1

### PROCEDURE

#### Removal

- Remove the fuel fill door adapter. Refer to the service information.

- Break the six lock tabs.

- Pry up the lock tab, then remove the mis-fuel inhibit device. NOTE: Never reuse the mis-fuel inhibit device.

- Set the capless wrench and slightly turn the main flap unit counterclockwise. NOTE: The main flap unit may move only a small amount. This is normal.

- Remove the capless wrench and install the flapper retainer onto the main flap unit as shown to keep the flap open.

- Insert the capless pick under the lock tab and release the lock by turning the pick counterclockwise. NOTE: The lock tab is unlocked when it folds inside the filler neck opening.

- Set the capless wrench onto the main flap unit as shown. NOTE: It may be necessary to move the flapper retainer slightly to install the capless wrench.

- Turn the main flap unit clockwise until it stops, then remove it. NOTE: If the lock tab was disengaged properly, the main flap unit should turn easily. If you feel resistance, remove the capless wrench and confirm that the lock tab was disengaged.

#### Installation

- Position the new main flap unit onto the filler neck with the top arrow at the 1 o'clock position.

- Install the main flap unit by doing the following: Set the capless wrench onto the main flap unit. While pressing down on the capless wrench, turn the main flap unit counterclockwise until you hear a click. The arrow on the main flap unit should face up. Slightly turn the capless wrench clockwise to confirm that the lock tab is engaged. The main flap unit should not turn.

- Set the capless wrench onto the main flap unit.

- While pressing down on the capless wrench, turn the main flap unit counterclockwise until you hear a click. The arrow on the main flap unit should face up.

- Slightly turn the capless wrench clockwise to confirm that the lock tab is engaged. The main flap unit should not turn.

- Install the new mis-fuel inhibit device.

- Reinstall the remaining removed parts.
````
